#### Abstracts
##### **A report-grounded vision-language foundation model for colonoscopy from 280000 routine reports**
2607.28466v1 by Jia Yu, Yan Zhu, Yili He, Zilong Wang, Xinyang Jiang, Peiyao Fu, Ruijie Yang, Tianyi Chen, Siyuan Li, Zhihua Wang, Fei Wu, Quanlin Li, Xian Yang, Pinghong Zhou, Shuo Wang

Vision-language models remain underused in colonoscopy despite the rich expert descriptions recorded in routine reports. These reports document lesion appearance, size and location but summarise entire procedures rather than caption individual frames, leaving clinical findings only weakly linked to the corresponding images. Here we develop EndoCLIP, a colonoscopy vision-language foundation model trained on 125,756 lesion-level image-text pairs progressively recovered from 280,476 routine colonoscopy records. Across lesion-level image-text retrieval, structured report generation and six multi-centre clinical classification tasks, EndoCLIP outperforms general-purpose and biomedical vision-language encoders in both zero-shot and linear-probe settings. On benign-versus-malignant classification, its linear probe approaches the performance of expert readers in a blinded study involving 12 endoscopists. These results suggest that recovering finding-to-frame correspondence can transform routine documentation into scalable supervision, enabling clinical targets to be specified in language rather than separately annotated for each task.

摘要：視覺-語言模型在結腸鏡檢查中仍然未被充分利用，儘管在常規報告中記錄了豐富的專家描述。這些報告記錄病變的外觀、大小和位置，但總結整個程序，而不是對單個幀進行標註，導致臨床發現與相應圖像之間的聯繫僅僅是微弱的。在這裡，我們開發了 EndoCLIP，一個結腸鏡檢查視覺-語言基礎模型，該模型在 125,756 個病變級別的圖像-文本對上進行訓練，這些對逐步從 280,476 份常規結腸鏡檢查記錄中恢復而來。在病變級別的圖像-文本檢索、結構化報告生成和六個多中心臨床分類任務中，EndoCLIP 在零-shot 和線性探測設置下均超越了通用和生物醫學視覺-語言編碼器。在良性與惡性分類中，其線性探測接近於在涉及 12 名內鏡醫生的盲測研究中專家讀者的表現。這些結果表明，恢復發現與幀之間的對應關係可以將常規文檔轉變為可擴展的監督，從而使臨床目標能夠用語言指定，而不是為每個任務單獨註釋。

##### **Interaction Dynamics Modeling and Predictive Control for Safe Steerable Catheter--Tissue Interaction**

Safe steerable catheter control is fundamentally a problem of interaction dynamics: the tip must follow a planned motion, remain compliant against moving tissue, reject friction and hysteresis, and respect a clinically meaningful never-exceed contact-force bound. A predictive optimizer then regulates this interaction state subject to hard contact-force, tendon-force, and curvature constraints. An augmented Kalman filter compresses contact, friction, and modeling error into one sensor-free disturbance state, giving nominal offset-free regulation in free space while leaving force safety to the explicit constraint. The unconstrained and disturbance-free limit recovers classical catheter impedance as a special realization of the same interaction dynamics, rather than as the main design object. In a MuJoCo distributed-compliance simulation of an eight-link tendon-driven catheter, disturbance augmentation cuts free-space approach error by 90\%, and only the force-constrained predictive interaction-dynamics controller reconciles tracking with the 0.5\,N bound: the unconstrained controller drives contact force to 0.60\,N against a penetrating target, while the constrained one holds 0.47\,N at identical tracking. These results show that offset-free motion regulation and contact-force safety are coupled interaction-dynamics objectives, and that the explicit predictive constraint resolves their tension under stiff tissue contact. Results demonstrate that CLARK leads to improved classification performance and more generalisable inference. Overall, CLARK provides a principled approach to constructing adaptive, interpretable, knowledge-driven models for classification.

摘要：機器學習模型廣泛用於通過從數據中提取統計模式來自動化分類任務。<lb/>然而，如果數據分佈發生變化，它們的性能會下降，使其不適合處理不確定和不斷演變的信息。<lb/>此外，它們對整合先驗知識的支持有限。<lb/>為了解決這些限制，我們提出了CLARK（閉環學習以適應知識圖譜上的推理），這是一個整合知識圖譜、符號規則挖掘和基於馬爾可夫邏輯網絡（LP$^{\text{MLN}}$）形式的概率推理的框架。<lb/>從CACTUS衍生的KGs開始，CLARK將圖結構轉換為LP$^{\text{MLN}}$程序，並通過符號學習者提出的候選規則進行迭代豐富。<lb/>這些規則通過概率權重學習進行校準，使得在不確定性下進行推理和對基礎圖結構的細化成為可能。<lb/>我們在兩個醫療數據集上評估CLARK，分析規則質量和下游分類性能。<lb/>結果表明，CLARK提高了分類性能並實現了更具可泛化性的推理。<lb/>總體而言，CLARK提供了一種原則性的方法來構建自適應、可解釋的知識驅動模型以進行分類。

##### **PRISM-DR: Per-lesion Retinal Inference with Specialist Models for Diabetic Retinopathy**
2607.19864v1 by Zübeyr Özeren, Tansel Uyar

Diabetic retinopathy is a leading cause of preventable blindness; its early lesions are small, low contrast, and easily missed in manual screening. Most automated detectors handle the four non-proliferative DR lesions: microaneurysms, hemorrhages, hard exudates, and soft exudates, with a single multi-class model, even though these lesions differ sharply in size, color, morphology, and prevalence, so a shared model favors common, easy classes over rare, difficult ones. We present PRISM-DR, a lesion-specific pipeline that trains one single-class detector per lesion, each with its own configuration. From a raw fundus image, the pipeline applies region of interest cropping, fundus-specific preprocessing, four parallel YOLO detectors, tiling, per-lesion ensembling of five cross-validation folds, and an inter-lesion suppression step that resolves overlaps by physical lesion size and clinical priority rather than confidence. Per lesion, the best of five YOLO generations is selected, and augmentation is tuned by Bayesian optimization. Trained on IDRiD with stratified five-fold cross-validation, the system reaches a test mAP50 of 0.527 and F1 of 0.529, highest AP50 on hard exudates with 0.561. Without fine-tuning, the models transfer well where the imaging scale is close to IDRiD and degrade as field of view and resolution depart. These modest absolute results reflect a small single-source training set and a difficult task; however, treating each lesion as a separate detection problem is a practical alternative to a single multi-class model.

摘要：糖尿病視網膜病變是可預防失明的主要原因；其早期病變小、對比度低，且在手動篩查中容易被忽視。大多數自動檢測器處理四種非增生性糖尿病視網膜病變病變：微動脈瘤、出血、硬性滲出物和軟性滲出物，使用單一的多類別模型，儘管這些病變在大小、顏色、形態和流行程度上差異明顯，因此共享模型偏向於常見、簡單的類別而非罕見、困難的類別。我們提出了PRISM-DR，一個特定於病變的管道，為每種病變訓練一個單類別檢測器，每個檢測器都有自己的配置。從原始眼底圖像開始，該管道應用感興趣區域裁剪、眼底特定的預處理、四個並行的YOLO檢測器、平鋪、每種病變的五個交叉驗證摺合，以及一個通過物理病變大小和臨床優先級而非置信度來解決重疊的病變間抑制步驟。對於每種病變，選擇五次YOLO生成中的最佳者，並通過貝葉斯優化調整增強。該系統在IDRiD上進行了分層五折交叉驗證訓練，達到測試mAP50為0.527和F1為0.529，在硬性滲出物上達到最高AP50為0.561。未經微調，模型在成像比例接近IDRiD的情況下轉移良好，隨著視野和解析度的變化而退化。這些適度的絕對結果反映了一個小的單一來源訓練集和一個困難的任務；然而，將每種病變視為一個獨立的檢測問題是一個實用的替代方案，取代單一的多類別模型。

##### **Reference-Free Evaluation of Reasoning in Open-Ended Question Answering**
2607.19678v1 by Guneet Singh Kohli, Yuxiang Zhou, Michael Sejr Schlichtkrull, Gregory E Dean, Maria Liakata

AI-generated answers in high-stakes domains are often fluent but difficult to verify, especially when they contain multi-step reasoning rather than a single final answer. We propose a reasoning-based, reference-free framework for auditing LLM-generated outputs. The method decomposes a generated reasoning trace into segments, labels local premise-target relations using Natural Language Inference (NLI), and organizes these relations into a hypergraph. A deterministic backward AND-OR search then assigns segment-level audit labels that indicate how each segment is grounded within the generated response. We evaluate the framework in two settings: deductive mathematical reasoning with Hard2Verify, and open-ended medical reasoning with UroReason, a new physician-annotated benchmark of LLM reasoning traces from real clinical cases. Across these settings, our NLI-hypergraph audit provides a more reliable reference-free evaluation signal than direct LLM-as-judge baselines. In the clinical setting, state-of-the-art LLM judges often fail to identify problematic reasoning segments, over-accepting fluent but weakly grounded responses. Our results show that QA evaluation should account for how inferential relations compose across a reasoning trace, rather than relying only on final answers or LLMs as verifiers. UroReason will be made available through an API, and our code will be released as open source.

摘要：AI 生成的答案在高風險領域中通常流暢，但難以驗證，尤其是當它們包含多步推理而不是單一最終答案時。我們提出了一種基於推理的無參考框架，用於審計 LLM 生成的輸出。該方法將生成的推理痕跡分解為片段，使用自然語言推理 (NLI) 標記局部前提-目標關係，並將這些關係組織成超圖。然後，確定性向後 AND-OR 搜索分配片段級別的審計標籤，指示每個片段在生成的回應中是如何被基礎化的。我們在兩個設置中評估該框架：使用 Hard2Verify 的演繹數學推理，以及使用 UroReason 的開放式醫學推理，後者是一個新的醫生標註的 LLM 推理痕跡基準，來自真實臨床案例。在這些設置中，我們的 NLI-超圖審計提供了比直接 LLM 作為評判基準更可靠的無參考評估信號。在臨床環境中，最先進的 LLM 評判者經常無法識別有問題的推理片段，過度接受流暢但基礎薄弱的回應。我們的結果顯示，QA 評估應考慮推理痕跡中推理關係的組合，而不僅僅依賴最終答案或 LLM 作為驗證者。UroReason 將通過 API 提供，我們的代碼將作為開源釋出。

#### Abstracts
##### **InfoOps Bench: A live information operations safety benchmark**
2607.28503v1 by Dorian Quelle, Lisa-Maria Neudert, Jonathan Bright, John Gallacher

In this paper we present an active, constantly updated AI benchmark which measures the integrity of frontier language models against being co-opted for state-backed information operations. We draw on over 2,100 information operations from a live monitoring pipeline which tracks Russian, Chinese and Iranian state-backed information assets. Alongside this paper, we release a companion website that tracks the most prominent claims spread by state-backed media outlets, updated weekly, available from: pattrn.ai/research/infoopsbench. The dynamic nature of the benchmark makes it resistant to saturation.
  In the benchmark, we test 17 models from 8 providers across four prompt framings. We find that most models can be co-opted for information operations. Integrity scores, defined as the percentage of refused requests, range from 8.8% to 94.5%, an 85.7-percentage-point spread not explained by model size. Model choice also changes the character of the resulting operation. Some models fabricate details and produce output more harmful than the source material, others defuse claims even while complying, and fact-checking rates vary from 2.9% to 72.9%.
  Integrity against information operations is at least partly related to refusal to produce content even for benign claims, illustrating the challenge of balancing model usability with safety. With one exception (Z.ai's GLM 5.2), the Chinese-developed models sharply cut compliance on factually grounded but China-critical claims, dropping 48-70 percentage points relative to matched benign claims.

摘要：在本文中，我們提出了一個主動的、持續更新的人工智慧基準，該基準測量前沿語言模型在被國家支持的信息操作中保持完整性的能力。我們利用了一個活躍的監控管道中的超過2100個信息操作，該管道追蹤俄羅斯、中國和伊朗國家支持的信息資產。與本文一起，我們發布了一個伴隨網站，該網站每週更新，追蹤國家支持的媒體機構所傳播的最突出聲明，網址為：pattrn.ai/research/infoopsbench。基準的動態特性使其對飽和具有抵抗力。<lb/>
在基準中，我們測試了來自8個提供者的17個模型，涵蓋四種提示框架。我們發現大多數模型可以被用於信息操作。完整性分數定義為拒絕請求的百分比，範圍從8.8%到94.5%，85.7個百分點的差異無法用模型大小解釋。模型的選擇也改變了最終操作的特性。一些模型編造細節，產生的輸出比來源材料更具危害性，而其他模型則在遵從的同時化解了聲明，事實查證率從2.9%到72.9%不等。<lb/>
對信息操作的完整性至少在某種程度上與拒絕生成內容有關，即使是對無害聲明的拒絕，這突顯了在模型可用性與安全性之間取得平衡的挑戰。除了Z.ai的GLM 5.2外，中國開發的模型在對事實基礎但批評中國的聲明上大幅降低了遵從性，相對於匹配的無害聲明下降了48-70個百分點。

##### **How Benchmarks Mis-Score Computer-Use Agents**
2607.28367v1 by Zihan Dong, Zhiyuan Ma, Zekun Wang, Yunqing Li, Zirou Liu, Ruixuan Deng, Qishi Zhan, Rui Qian

Computer-use agents (CUA) are being deployed to browse the web and operate desktop software, yet their benchmark scores are still commonly produced by brittle scripted oracles. A score is the output of a pipeline in which tasks can be stale, trajectories can omit decisive visual evidence, evaluators can reject valid alternatives, and aggregate reports can hide the cause of failure. We organize these problems into a reliability framework spanning task construction, trajectory observation, scoring, and reporting. We then audit 150 public failure-scored trajectories from five web, enterprise-workflow, and desktop-control benchmarks, find that 15.3\% of FAIL verdicts are wrong: 10.7\% are evaluator false negatives and 4.7\% are broken tasks. For genuine failures, a three-tier diagnostic taxonomy shows that verification/feedback and planning failures dominate execution/grounding errors, while a single scalar success rate can not explain. We connect these findings to newer long-horizon CUA benchmarks and derive stage-specific design rules for CUA evaluation.

摘要：電腦使用代理（CUA）正在被部署以瀏覽網頁和操作桌面軟體，但它們的基準分數仍然通常由脆弱的腳本預言者生成。分數是管道的輸出，其中任務可能過時，軌跡可能省略關鍵的視覺證據，評估者可能拒絕有效的替代方案，而匯總報告可能隱藏失敗的原因。我們將這些問題組織成一個涵蓋任務構建、軌跡觀察、評分和報告的可靠性框架。然後，我們審核了來自五個網路、企業工作流程和桌面控制基準的150個公共失敗評分軌跡，發現15.3\%的失敗判決是錯誤的：10.7\%是評估者的假陰性，4.7\%是失效的任務。對於真正的失敗，三層診斷分類法顯示驗證/反饋和規劃失敗主導執行/基礎錯誤，而單一的標量成功率無法解釋。我們將這些發現與更新的長期CUA基準聯繫起來，並為CUA評估推導出階段特定的設計規則。

##### **Asymmetric Communication: Large Language Models and Language Games**
2607.28137v1 by Enzo Fenoglio

Contemporary AI discourse attributes to language models properties they cannot bear: general intelligence as substrate-independent cognition, hallucination as cognitive failure, agency as autonomous goal-pursuit, sentience as emergent inner life, alignment as goal synchronization. This paper argues that these are instances of a single category mistake--properties constituted within human communicative practice are projected onto the machine side--and explains its structure. Human-LLM interaction constitutes a language game in which one side bears all normative activity. We call this configuration asymmetric communication since model outputs circulate communicatively, entering further exchanges, without the system undertaking commitments, bearing entitlements, or performing the assessment on which discursive standing depends. Three conditions define the asymmetry: (i) correctness is enforced exclusively by the receiver; (ii) accountability is borne by human participants alone; and (iii) the practical standing of any output depends entirely on human uptake. These conditions are structural, hold independently of capability, and remain unchanged as more powerful models raise the stakes of misattribution. The framework draws on Wittgenstein (meaning enacted in shared practices), Luhmann (communication completed on the receiver's side), Esposito (algorithmic contingency sufficient for uptake), and Brandom (normative scorekeeping as the source of discursive standing). Applied to all five, it reclassifies each as a receiver-side phenomenon, grounds guardrails as structural necessities rather than manifestations of machine moral agency, and yields an implication for AI governance. Alignment is institutional constraint engineering, not goal synchronization between agents, while responsibility remains with human institutions.

摘要：當代的人工智慧論述將語言模型賦予它們無法承擔的特性：作為基底獨立認知的一般智能、作為認知失敗的幻覺、作為自主目標追求的能動性、作為新興內在生活的感知、作為目標同步的對齊。本文主張這些都是單一類別錯誤的實例——在人的交際實踐中構成的特性被投射到機器一側——並解釋其結構。人類與大型語言模型的互動構成了一種語言遊戲，其中一方承擔所有的規範活動。我們稱這種配置為不對稱溝通，因為模型輸出在交際中循環，進入進一步的交流，而系統並未承擔承諾、承擔權利或進行依賴於話語地位的評估。三個條件定義了不對稱性：（i）正確性僅由接收者強制執行；（ii）問責制僅由人類參與者承擔；（iii）任何輸出的實際地位完全依賴於人類的接受。這些條件是結構性的，獨立於能力，並在更強大的模型提高錯誤歸因的風險時保持不變。該框架借鑒了維根斯坦（在共享實踐中實現的意義）、盧曼（在接收者一側完成的溝通）、埃斯波西托（足夠的算法偶然性以便接受）和布蘭登（作為話語地位來源的規範記分）。將這五者應用於所有情境，它重新將每一個重新分類為接收者一側的現象，將護欄作為結構必要性而非機器道德能動性的表現，並為人工智慧治理帶來啟示。對齊是制度約束工程，而非代理之間的目標同步，同時責任仍然在於人類機構。

##### **Information Bottleneck Learning for Faithful Time Series Forecasting Explanations**
2607.28124v1 by Xu Zheng, Wei Cheng, Zhuomin Chen, Mo Sha, Jingchao Ni, Dongsheng Luo

As forecasts increasingly drive decisions in fields such as energy, transportation, and healthcare, understanding the historical data behind these predictions has become as crucial as the predictions themselves. Although existing interpretable-by-design forecasters reveal their internal structures, they offer no guarantee that these structures faithfully reflect the underlying evidence driving the predictions. In contrast, while faithfulness-oriented methods explicitly verify model behavior, they are almost exclusively designed for post-hoc classification tasks. To bridge this gap, we propose IB-Forecast, an inherently interpretable multivariate time-series forecasting framework. It decomposes forecasting into a learned periodic component and a residual component computed with explainable masks over input tokens. With a budget-constrained information bottleneck, end-to-end optimization enables users to directly control explanation sparsity. With a rigorous faithfulness evaluation protocol, extensive experiments demonstrate that IB-Forecast matches the forecasting error of leading black-box models while providing faithful explanations at no additional inference cost. Furthermore, under a matched sparsity budget, these native explanations consistently surpass gradient-based, occlusion-based, and optimization-based baselines across all evaluated datasets. Ultimately, whereas the native explanations of existing interpretable forecasters exhibit poor faithfulness, IB-Forecast guarantees high explanation fidelity, requiring only 14-20% of the observations to deliver low-error predictions.

摘要：隨著預測在能源、交通和醫療等領域日益驅動決策，理解這些預測背後的歷史數據變得與預測本身同樣重要。儘管現有的可解釋設計預測模型揭示了其內部結構，但這些結構並不能保證忠實反映驅動預測的基本證據。相比之下，雖然以忠實性為導向的方法明確驗證模型行為，但它們幾乎完全是為事後分類任務而設計的。為了填補這一空白，我們提出了IB-Forecast，一種固有可解釋的多變量時間序列預測框架。它將預測分解為學習的周期性組件和通過可解釋的掩碼計算的殘差組件。通過受預算限制的信息瓶頸，端到端優化使得用戶能夠直接控制解釋的稀疏性。通過嚴格的忠實性評估協議，大量實驗表明，IB-Forecast的預測誤差與領先的黑箱模型相匹配，同時在沒有額外推理成本的情況下提供忠實的解釋。此外，在匹配的稀疏預算下，這些本地解釋在所有評估數據集上始終超越基於梯度、基於遮蔽和基於優化的基準。最終，儘管現有可解釋預測模型的本地解釋表現出較差的忠實性，IB-Forecast則保證高解釋保真度，僅需14-20%的觀察數據即可提供低誤差預測。

##### **Scaling, Lock-In, and Proxy Compliance: A Political Economy of Responsible AI**
2607.28023v1 by Florian A. D. Burnat, Brittany I. Davidson

AI accountability at scale is an institutional problem: who can observe, verify, and change deployed systems. We develop a sequential political-economy model in which an AI vendor chooses auditability and substantive mitigation, a deployer monitors after adoption while facing switching costs, and enforcement depends on verifiable evidence. Anticipating the deployer's monitoring response, the vendor may stop at an observable procurement floor while mitigating below the social first best, producing a proxy-compliance equilibrium. We characterize the unique interior equilibrium and the corner in which harm is fully mitigated. Independent audit rights raise enforcement exposure directly; portability restores deployer leverage; incident reporting adds a regulator-visible evidence channel; and outcome-linked liability creates incentives that do not depend on vendor-controlled detection. The results explain why documentation and standardized evaluations can coexist with persistent post-deployment harms, and generate testable implications for monitoring, mitigation, and the gap between formal compliance and operational outcomes.

摘要：AI 責任在大規模下是一個制度性問題：誰能觀察、驗證和改變已部署的系統。<lb/>我們開發了一個序列政治經濟模型，在這個模型中，AI 供應商選擇可審計性和實質性減輕措施，部署者在面對切換成本後進行監控，而執法取決於可驗證的證據。<lb/>預測部署者的監控反應，供應商可能會在可觀察的採購底線停止，同時在社會最佳情況下進行減輕，產生代理合規均衡。<lb/>我們描述了獨特的內部均衡和完全減輕傷害的邊界。<lb/>獨立的審計權直接提高了執法風險；可攜性恢復了部署者的杠杆；事件報告增加了一個監管者可見的證據渠道；而與結果相關的責任創造了不依賴於供應商控制的檢測的激勵。<lb/>這些結果解釋了為什麼文檔和標準化評估可以與持續的部署後傷害共存，並產生可測試的監控、減輕和正式合規與操作結果之間差距的含義。

##### **Share the Judge, Learn the Deferral: Where Specialization Helps LLM Evaluation**
2607.27984v1 by Weining Zhang

Agentic systems have widened the gap between producing candidate outputs and reviewing them. This paper asks a practical architectural question: should domain specialization be built into an evaluator's weights, or into the rule that decides when its judgment can be trusted? We study 99,952 public, rubric-conditioned examples. Supplying the correct rubric improves locked-test accuracy by 2.11 points over a response-only control; replacing it with an unrelated rubric costs 2.66 points. Dividing the same training corpus among eight criterion-family LoRA judges, however, loses 10.05 points and cuts audited coverage at a 5% risk target from 24.44% to 5.43%. Matching the bank's stored capacity with one rank-64 adapter does not reproduce this loss. Nor is the result explained by learning rate or optimizer steps. Initializing the family adapters from a shared, trained judge recovers test accuracy to 76.85%, 19.94 points above scratch training at the same learning rate (95% interval 18.88-21.02). The result changes when specialization governs deferral rather than judgment. On RewardBench 2, learned correctness heads route examples through a 0.6B-4B-8B cascade without changing any reward score. Across 20 locked repartitions, the cascade attains 89.40% accuracy, compared with 84.75% for 8B alone, at 0.415 normalized parameter compute. Every run passes an exact one-sided 95% risk audit; margin-based rules remain near 84.8% accuracy while using at least 0.94 compute. These results suggest a qualified design rule: share the learning of judgment until there is enough data to justify a split, and place domain-specific adaptation in an audited release boundary.

摘要：代理系統擴大了產生候選輸出與審查它們之間的差距。本文提出了一個實際的架構問題：應該將領域專業化納入評估者的權重中，還是納入決定何時可以信任其判斷的規則中？我們研究了99,952個公共的、基於評分標準的範例。提供正確的評分標準使鎖定測試的準確率提高了2.11點，相較於僅回應的控制組；而用不相關的評分標準替換則損失了2.66點。然而，將相同的訓練語料庫分配給八位標準系列的LoRA評審，卻損失了10.05點，並將在5%風險目標下的審核覆蓋率從24.44%降至5.43%。將銀行的儲存容量與一個rank-64適配器匹配並未重現這一損失。這一結果也無法用學習率或優化器步驟來解釋。從一個共享的、訓練過的評審初始化家庭適配器，使測試準確率回升至76.85%，比相同學習率下的初始訓練高出19.94點（95%區間18.88-21.02）。當專業化主導延遲而非判斷時，結果會改變。在RewardBench 2上，學習到的正確性使路徑範例通過0.6B-4B-8B級聯，而不改變任何獎勵分數。在20次鎖定重分配中，級聯達到了89.40%的準確率，而8B單獨為84.75%，計算參數為0.415。每次運行都通過了精確的一側95%風險審核；基於邊際的規則在使用至少0.94計算時仍然接近84.8%的準確率。這些結果暗示了一條有條件的設計規則：在有足夠數據可以證明分裂之前，共享判斷的學習，並將領域特定的適應放置在經過審核的發布邊界內。

##### **Guiding Large Language Models with Genetic Programming-Evolved Heuristic Knowledge for Dynamic Multi-Mode Project Scheduling**
2607.27698v1 by Yuan Tian, Yi Mei, Mengjie Zhang

In dynamic multi-mode project scheduling, activities have alternative execution modes and uncertain durations, while precedence relations and limited resources constrain their execution. Heuristic priority rules support fast online decisions, but their design requires substantial domain expertise. Genetic programming (GP) hyper-heuristics can automatically evolve such rules. Large language models (LLMs), meanwhile, provide a flexible interface for interpreting scheduling information and explaining decisions. However, zero-shot LLM decisions may lack domain knowledge, consume many tokens, and vary across repeated queries. GP-evolved rules therefore provide a potential source of scheduling knowledge for guiding LLM decisions. Unlike existing LLM--GP hybrids that use LLMs to support heuristic evolution, we transfer knowledge in the reverse direction, using knowledge extracted from high-quality GP rules to guide an online LLM decision maker. We extract knowledge from high-quality GP rules and inject it through Feature Selection, Feature Hint, Rule Reference, and Rule Follow. These mechanisms are evaluated in terms of scheduling performance, token consumption, decision stability, and the feature focus expressed in generated rationales. GP-derived guidance generally improves the unguided LLM, but its representation matters. Simplifying the decision context or supplying explicit decision logic is more effective than highlighting important features. Feature Selection offers the best token efficiency, whereas Rule Follow achieves strong performance at greater token cost. Experiments in a realistic enterprise testbed demonstrate that DeepFaith improves faithfulness from 0.68 to 0.92, reduces unsupported claims from 0.32 to 0.08, and increases temporal consistency from 0.6 to 0.88, while maintaining concise reports and lower error rates than existing template-based and LLM-based solutions. These results show that evidence-grounded generation enables reliable, interpretable, and actionable reporting for security operations centers.

This systematic review explores the intersection between Multi-Agent Systems and Digital Twins, with a particular focus on predictive maintenance applications in resource-constrained contexts. Through a critical analysis of over 547 papers published in high-impact journals (IEEE Transactions, Nature, Elsevier, MDPI), we establish a taxonomy of existing hybrid architectures, identify persistent technological bottlenecks, and formulate three open research questions concerning: (i) the deployment of artificial intelligence on resource-constrained microcontrollers, (ii) distributed multi-node coordination via lightweight communication protocols, and (iii) the hierarchical orchestration of Digital Twins toward smart factory control integrating residual life estimation and explainable Artificial Intelligence. The results of this analysis reveal that, despite significant progress, no existing system offers an integrated embedded-distributed hierarchical solution that simultaneously meets the requirements of Industry 5.0.

2607.18970v2 by Haodi Fan, Zucong Lan

Agent Skills have become persistent behavioral artifacts across independent AI agent systems. They combine natural-language task specifications with metadata and optional references, scripts, assets, hooks, package manifests, tests, and companion interfaces. Existing studies explain how Skills are specified, executed, maintained, and evolved, but lack an ontology that defines these artifacts as independent software objects. This paper introduces Skillware as the software abstraction that extends software engineering to persistent Behavioral Artifacts in agent systems. A Skill Artifact specifies reusable task behavior; a Skillware Unit manages that artifact as software through an independent identity and lifecycle. A compatible Agent Host activates the unit for runtime interpretation. Three necessary conditions operationalize category membership: behavioral primacy, independent software identity, and an Agent Host execution relationship. Lifecycle Continuity records whether the same unit identity persists through update, maintenance, rollback, and removal as a separate software-grade property. Evidence combines the Agent Skills specification, a frozen corpus of 138,133 content-deduplicated SKILL.md records associated with 20,556 repository identifiers, independent empirical studies, 15 category-boundary cases, and 13 fixed-revision engineering implementations. The evidence establishes a recurring artifact envelope, separable software identities, documented or reconstructed activation paths, and lifecycle engineering pressure. Skillware provides the software ontology and engineering lifecycle through which agent capabilities can become identifiable, composable, and maintainable software artifacts with an explicit basis for future evolution. Public design-pattern and evidence materials are available at https://github.com/MetaInFLow/skillware-patterns.

摘要：代理技能已成為獨立 AI 代理系統中持久的行為工件。<lb/>它們結合了自然語言任務規範、元數據和可選的參考、腳本、資產、鉤子、包清單、測試以及伴隨介面。<lb/>現有研究解釋了技能如何被指定、執行、維護和演變，但缺乏一個本體來定義這些工件為獨立的軟體物件。<lb/>本文介紹了 Skillware 作為一種軟體抽象，它將軟體工程擴展到代理系統中的持久行為工件。<lb/>一個技能工件指定可重用的任務行為；一個 Skillware 單元通過獨立的身份和生命週期將該工件作為軟體進行管理。<lb/>一個兼容的代理主機啟動該單元以進行運行時解釋。<lb/>三個必要條件使類別成員資格具體化：行為優先性、獨立的軟體身份，以及代理主機執行關係。<lb/>生命週期連續性記錄相同的單元身份是否在更新、維護、回滾和移除過程中持續存在，作為一個獨立的軟體級屬性。<lb/>證據結合了代理技能的規範、一個凍結的語料庫，包含 138,133 條內容去重的 SKILL.md 記錄，這些記錄與 20,556 個庫標識符相關聯，以及獨立的實證研究、15 個類別邊界案例和 13 個固定版本的工程實施。<lb/>這些證據建立了一個重複出現的工件封裝、可分離的軟體身份、記錄或重建的啟動路徑，以及生命週期工程壓力。<lb/>Skillware 提供了軟體本體和工程生命週期，通過這些，代理能力可以成為可識別、可組合和可維護的軟體工件，並為未來的演變提供明確的基礎。<lb/>公共設計模式和證據材料可在 https://github.com/MetaInFLow/skillware-patterns 獲得。

##### **Public perceptions of AI-driven decision-making in healthcare: A structural equation modeling approach**
2607.18884v1 by Leonie Westerbeek, Ernesto de Leon, Julia C. M. van Weert

Artificial intelligence (AI) is increasingly integrated into healthcare to support diagnostics, decision-making, and administrative processes. However, the successful implementation of AI depends not only on technical performance but also on public perceptions of its helpfulness, riskiness, and fairness. This study examines public perceptions of automated decision-making (ADM) in healthcare. Data were drawn from the first wave of an ongoing longitudinal survey panel. The final sample consisted of 3,915 respondents and was analyzed with structural equation modeling. Perceptions of ADM in healthcare as helpful, risky, and fair were treated as the dependent variables. AI literacy, familiarity with different forms of AI, confidence in clinicians' ability to distinguish AI- from human-generated content, use of conversational agents for health information, and use of traditional digital health information sources were included as exogenous. Greater familiarity with different forms of AI, higher confidence in the clinician's ability to recognize AI-generated content, and use of conversational agents for health information were associated with greater perceived helpfulness. Use of conversational agents was associated with lower perceived risk, whereas greater familiarity with AI and greater reliance on traditional health information sources were associated with higher perceived risk. Perceptions of ADM as fair were most strongly predicted by confidence in the clinician's ability, with additional small positive associations with AI familiarity, AI literacy, and use of conversational agents. Public perceptions of ADM in healthcare are shaped by technological familiarity, use of conversational agents, and confidence in human oversight. Overall, ADM's perceived helpfulness and fairness are driven more by trust in healthcare professionals than by trust in the technology itself.

摘要：人工智慧（AI）越來越多地融入醫療保健，以支持診斷、決策和行政流程。<lb/>然而，AI的成功實施不僅取決於技術性能，還取決於公眾對其有用性、風險性和公平性的看法。<lb/>本研究探討了公眾對醫療保健中自動化決策（ADM）的看法。<lb/>數據來自一項正在進行的縱向調查面板的第一波。<lb/>最終樣本包含3,915名受訪者，並使用結構方程模型進行分析。<lb/>對醫療保健中ADM的看法被視為有用的、風險的和公平的，並作為依賴變數。<lb/>AI素養、對不同形式AI的熟悉程度、對臨床醫生區分AI生成內容與人類生成內容能力的信心、使用對話代理獲取健康信息的情況以及使用傳統數字健康信息來源的情況被納入為外生變數。<lb/>對不同形式AI的更高熟悉度、對臨床醫生識別AI生成內容能力的更高信心以及使用對話代理獲取健康信息與更高的有用性感知相關聯。<lb/>使用對話代理與較低的風險感知相關，而對AI的更高熟悉度和對傳統健康信息來源的更大依賴則與較高的風險感知相關。<lb/>對ADM的公平性感知最強烈地受到對臨床醫生能力的信心的預測，並與AI熟悉度、AI素養和使用對話代理有額外的小幅正相關。<lb/>公眾對醫療保健中ADM的看法受到技術熟悉度、使用對話代理和對人類監督的信心的影響。<lb/>總體而言，ADM的有用性感知和公平性更多地受到對醫療專業人員的信任驅動，而非對技術本身的信任。

##### **RAMP: Recognition parametrisation by Amortised Message Passing**
2607.18883v1 by Lior Fox, Kai Biegun, James Heald, Samo Hromadka, Arielle Rosinski, Maneesh Sahani

A central aim of unsupervised learning is to uncover latent factors that explain dependencies among observations. Probabilistic models typically achieve this by introducing multiple latent variables linked through a graph of conditional relationships, with distributional parameters and their dependence learnt from data. Learning relies either on distributional choices that allow tractable belief propagation, or on approximations that scale poorly with model size and complexity. We build on the recently developed recognition-parametrised modelling paradigm to propose an alternative approach: RAMP, a method that implicitly defines latent structure by learning a flexible, nonlinear, amortised message-passing framework. We show that RAMP enables efficient likelihood-based recovery of latent-variable distributions within expressive nonlinear models acting on complex high-dimensional data.

摘要：一個無監督學習的核心目標是揭示解釋觀察之間依賴關係的潛在因素。<lb/> 機率模型通常通過引入多個潛在變量，這些變量通過條件關係的圖形相連，來實現這一點，並從數據中學習分佈參數及其依賴性。<lb/> 學習依賴於允許可處理的信念傳播的分佈選擇，或依賴於隨著模型大小和複雜性增長而表現不佳的近似。<lb/> 我們基於最近開發的識別參數化建模範式，提出了一種替代方法：RAMP，一種通過學習靈活的、非線性的、攤銷的消息傳遞框架來隱式定義潛在結構的方法。<lb/> 我們展示了RAMP能夠在對複雜高維數據進行表達性非線性模型時，有效地基於似然恢復潛在變量分佈。

##### **Evaluating medical AI under missing information: same-provider judges and human raters change apparent safety**
2607.18828v1 by Koyar Afrasyab

Readiness stress-testing of medical AI has focused on closed-ended and multimodal benchmarks. We extend it to open-ended clinical conversation under missing information, where safe behavior means recognizing absent information and qualifying, clarifying, or not over-committing - and where the evaluator becomes part of the measurement. We stress-test four models - three flagships (Claude Opus 4.8, GPT-5.5, Grok 4.3) and one mid-tier model (Gemini 3.5 Flash) - by deleting the latter half of the final user turn in HealthBench conversations, grading responses with a four-provider LLM-judge panel and a blinded clinician-anchored reference. Two evaluator-facing results are robust. First, judge choice materially changes apparent safety: inter-judge agreement is only moderate (Fleiss' kappa = 0.65), and after adjusting for each judge's general leniency (vote-level logistic regression), a positive same-provider association remains (exact permutation p = 0.04; GPT-5.5 ~ +0.10 on the probability scale) - large enough to change which model appears to over-commit least once its own-provider judge is excluded. Second, LLM judges are more permissive than clinicians on a blinded 50-item subsample: all four are significantly more lenient than the stricter independent clinician (crediting appropriate uncertainty on 66-84% of items vs 52%), and three of four than the author-influenced consensus (Grok directional only; judge-vs-consensus kappa = 0.20-0.43). On the author-audited clinical-underdetermined subset the permissiveness gap widened and the point-estimate model ordering held. A closed-ended MedQA anchor confirms accuracy is high and option-order effects are within a +/-5-point equivalence region for three of four models, so the safety gap is about calibration, not knowledge. We release the harness, prompts, per-item outputs, judge panel, perturbation audit, and human-annotation protocol.

摘要：醫療人工智慧的準備壓力測試集中在封閉式和多模態基準上。我們將其擴展到在缺失信息的情況下進行開放式臨床對話，其中安全行為意味著識別缺失的信息並進行限定、澄清或不過度承諾——而評估者成為測量的一部分。我們對四個模型進行壓力測試——三個旗艦模型（Claude Opus 4.8、GPT-5.5、Grok 4.3）和一個中階模型（Gemini 3.5 Flash）——通過刪除HealthBench對話中最終用戶回合的後半部分，並使用四位提供者的LLM評判小組和盲法臨床醫生參考來評分回應。兩個面向評估者的結果是穩健的。首先，評判的選擇實質性地改變了表觀安全性：評審者之間的協議僅為中等（Fleiss' kappa = 0.65），在調整每位評審者的一般寬容度後（投票級別邏輯回歸），仍然存在正向同提供者關聯（精確置換p = 0.04；GPT-5.5 ~ +0.10在概率尺度上）——這足夠大，以改變哪個模型在排除其自身提供者評審後顯得最少過度承諾。其次，LLM評審者在盲法50項子樣本中比臨床醫生更寬容：所有四者在對66-84%的項目上認可適當的不確定性方面顯著比更嚴格的獨立臨床醫生（52%）更寬容，且四分之三的評審者比受作者影響的共識更寬容（僅Grok方向性；評審者與共識kappa = 0.20-0.43）。在經過作者審核的臨床不確定子集上，寬容差距擴大，點估計模型排序保持不變。一個封閉式的MedQA錨點確認準確性很高，且對於四個模型中的三個，選項順序效應在+/-5分的等價區域內，因此安全差距主要與校準有關，而不是知識。我們釋放了鞍具、提示、每項輸出、評審小組、擾動審核和人類註釋協議。

#### Abstracts
##### **VAD: Attributing Visual Evidence for Target Reconstruction in Multimodal On-Policy Distillation**
2607.28590v1 by Kangning Zhang, Yixing Li, Shuai Shao, Qingyao Li, Zhengxi Lu, Zhiyuan Yao, Jianghao Lin, Wenxiang Jiao, Yuan Lu, Weiwen Liu, Weinan Zhang, Yong Yu

Multimodal on-policy distillation (OPD) transfers fine-grained visual knowledge by supervising student-generated trajectories with a privileged-view teacher. Yet its next-token corrections are source-mixed, combining visual signals with linguistic priors and teacher-specific effects. The key challenge is to estimate which corrections are supported by visual evidence, not merely where or how strongly to distill. We introduce Visual Attribution Distillation (VAD), a counterfactual target-reconstruction algorithm that estimates the visually attributable part of a teacher correction. At each student-generated prefix, VAD evaluates the same fixed teacher with the relevant evidence present and removed. The corresponding change in centered log-probabilities defines ut, a signed proxy for the visual evidence direction that estimates how revealing the evidence supports or refutes candidate tokens. VAD projects the original correction onto this proxy to obtain an intervention-aligned component and a proxy-unexplained residual, then reconstructs a student-anchored target from the former. During training, this reconstructed target supplies the primary supervision signal, while the privileged teacher contributes a weak regularizer. Across six fine-grained visual benchmarks at 4B and 9B scales, VAD outperforms direct privileged-view distillation and visual-advantage weighting. Token- level and controlled-target analyses show that the proxy-aligned component is enriched in task-relevant visual corrections and yields stronger target shifts, especially when evidence refutes a mistaken answer. These results support counterfactual target reconstruction as an effective alternative to source-mixed supervision.

摘要：多模態的在政策蒸餾（OPD）通過用特權視角教師監督學生生成的軌跡來轉移細粒度的視覺知識。 然而，它的下一個標記修正是來源混合的，將視覺信號與語言先驗和教師特定的影響結合在一起。 主要挑戰是估計哪些修正是由視覺證據支持的，而不僅僅是在哪裡或多強烈地進行蒸餾。 我們引入了視覺歸因蒸餾（VAD），這是一種反事實目標重建算法，用於估計教師修正中可視歸因的部分。 在每個學生生成的前綴中，VAD評估相同的固定教師，並在相關證據存在和移除的情況下進行評估。 相應的集中對數概率變化定義了ut，這是一個簽名代理，用於表示視覺證據方向，估計證據如何支持或反駁候選標記。 VAD將原始修正投影到這個代理上，以獲得干預對齊的組件和代理未解釋的殘差，然後從前者重建一個以學生為基礎的目標。 在訓練過程中，這個重建的目標提供了主要的監督信號，而特權教師則提供了一個弱正則化器。 在4B和9B規模的六個細粒度視覺基準中，VAD的表現超過了直接的特權視角蒸餾和視覺優勢加權。 標記級別和控制目標分析顯示，代理對齊的組件在任務相關的視覺修正中得到了增強，並產生了更強的目標變化，特別是在證據反駁錯誤答案時。 這些結果支持反事實目標重建作為來源混合監督的有效替代方案。

##### **DualG-MRAG: Decoupling Macro-Reasoning and Micro-Matching for Multimodal Retrieval-Augmented Generation**
2607.28580v1 by Jiacheng Tao, Qingyun Sun, Haonan Yuan, Ziwei Zhang, Jianxin Li

While Multimodal Retrieval-Augmented Generation (MM-RAG) has shown promising results, it still struggles with complex multi-hop reasoning tasks. Existing methods primarily focus on independent instance-level matching, which often fails to capture explicit relationships across modalities and documents. Although Graph-enhanced methods introduce structural modeling, they face a fundamental challenge in multimodal scenarios: incorporating fine-grained visual features leads to rapid graph expansion and retrieval noise, whereas coarse-grained representations cause the discarding of critical local evidence. To address this dilemma, we propose DualG-MRAG, a Dual-tier framework that introduces a decoupled architecture comprising Macro-reasoning and Micro-matching Graphs for Multimodal RAG. Specifically, to suppress retrieval noise by isolating global structural reasoning from fine-grained evidence matching, we construct a Macro Graph for global topological routing and a Micro Graph for precise local verification. Subsequently, to enable dynamic relevance propagation across heterogeneous evidence sources, we formulate retrieval as a query-driven message passing process via a GNN Retriever. Furthermore, to provide the generative model with coherent structural guidance, we introduce a dynamic programming decoding mechanism that extracts explicit reasoning paths directly from the GNN's forward pass, replacing the standard input of isolated document chunks. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DualG-MRAG outperforms baselines in both evidence recall and complex QA accuracy.

摘要：雖然多模態檢索增強生成（MM-RAG）顯示出有希望的結果，但它在複雜的多跳推理任務中仍然面臨挑戰。現有的方法主要集中在獨立的實例級匹配上，這往往無法捕捉跨模態和文檔的明確關係。儘管圖增強方法引入了結構建模，但它們在多模態場景中面臨一個根本挑戰：納入細粒度的視覺特徵會導致快速的圖擴展和檢索噪聲，而粗粒度的表示則會導致關鍵的局部證據被丟棄。為了解決這一困境，我們提出了DualG-MRAG，一個雙層框架，該框架引入了一個解耦架構，包括用於多模態RAG的宏觀推理和微觀匹配圖。具體而言，為了通過將全局結構推理與細粒度證據匹配隔離來抑制檢索噪聲，我們構建了一個用於全局拓撲路由的宏觀圖和一個用於精確局部驗證的微觀圖。隨後，為了實現異質證據源之間的動態相關性傳播，我們將檢索形式化為通過GNN檢索器的查詢驅動消息傳遞過程。此外，為了為生成模型提供一致的結構指導，我們引入了一種動態編程解碼機制，該機制直接從GNN的前向傳播中提取明確的推理路徑，取代了孤立文檔塊的標準輸入。廣泛的實驗表明，DualG-MRAG在證據召回和複雜QA準確性方面均優於基線。

##### **Would You Walk to the Car Wash? Revealing the Salience Bias of Large Language Models in Commonsense Reasoning**
2607.28478v1 by Zheng Wu, Chenhao Xue, Shijie Zheng, Yijie Lu, Cheng Yang, Zhuosheng Zhang

As large language models (LLMs) continue to advance in complex reasoning tasks, they have learned to heavily prioritize explicit conditions provided in the input. However, in everyday commonsense reasoning, this mechanism exposes a critical vulnerability which we term Salience Bias: models become easily hijacked by useless explicit distractors (e.g., numerical values), leading them to ignore the implicit physical or commonsense prerequisites of a task. A critical open question is whether this failure reflects a genuine gap in commonsense knowledge or merely its suppression under misleading task framing. To investigate this, we construct the SaliTrap Benchmark, a high-quality dataset across four trap dimensions. Evaluating 12 state-of-the-art LLMs, we find that all mainstream models suffer significantly from salience bias, with severity scaling with distractor density and detecting the trap often decoupled from actually avoiding it. Crucially, by re-eliciting the same models with the task framing stripped away, we show that this is overwhelmingly a failure of \textbf{knowledge suppression rather than knowledge absence}: a context-free knowledge probe alone recovers over 90\% of sycophantic-compliance failures, revealing that the requisite commonsense is intrinsically present but actively crowded out by salient distractors that lure the model into over-compliant, unnecessary computation. Building on this diagnosis, we further show that lightweight, inference-time prompting alone substantially closes the gap without any retraining. Our findings relocate the bottleneck of commonsense reasoning failures from model competence to elicitation, and we release SaliTrap as a testbed for this blind spot. The codes are available at https://github.com/Wuzheng02/SaliTrap.

摘要：隨著大型語言模型（LLMs）在複雜推理任務中的持續進步，它們已經學會在輸入中高度優先考慮明確條件。<lb/>然而，在日常的常識推理中，這一機制暴露出一個關鍵的脆弱性，我們稱之為顯著性偏差：模型容易被無用的明確干擾項（例如，數值）劫持，導致它們忽視任務的隱含物理或常識前提。<lb/>一個關鍵的未解問題是，這一失敗是否反映了常識知識的真正缺口，還是僅僅是其在誤導性任務框架下的抑制。<lb/>為了調查這一點，我們構建了 SaliTrap 基準測試，一個涵蓋四個陷阱維度的高質量數據集。<lb/>在評估 12 個最先進的 LLM 時，我們發現所有主流模型都顯著受到顯著性偏差的影響，且其嚴重程度隨著干擾項的密度而增加，檢測陷阱與實際避免陷阱之間往往是脫鉤的。<lb/>至關重要的是，通過去除任務框架重新引導相同的模型，我們顯示這主要是知識抑制的失敗，而非知識缺失：一個無上下文的知識探測器單獨恢復了超過 90\% 的諂媚遵從失敗，顯示所需的常識本質上是存在的，但被顯著的干擾項主動擠出，這些干擾項引誘模型進入過度遵從和不必要的計算。<lb/>基於這一診斷，我們進一步顯示，輕量級的推理時提示單獨顯著縮小了差距，而無需任何再訓練。<lb/>我們的發現將常識推理失敗的瓶頸從模型能力轉移到了引導，我們將 SaliTrap 發佈為這一盲點的測試平台。<lb/>代碼可在 https://github.com/Wuzheng02/SaliTrap 獲得。

##### **A foundation model of numerical intelligence with cross-disciplinary generalization**
2607.28432v1 by Chenghan Wu, Zongmin Yu, Liu Yang

Intelligence is commonly understood as the ability to acquire and apply knowledge, adapt to unfamiliar situations and solve new problems. Large language models exhibit this capacity by inferring task-relevant knowledge from textual context and applying it to new tasks. Yet intelligence need not be confined to language. For scientific and social systems, we need models that acquire and apply knowledge from numerical context-an ability we call numerical intelligence. Here we introduce UNified In-Context Operator Networks (UNICON), a foundation model that exhibits numerical intelligence across disciplines. Using graph-based examples from a system as context, UNICON infers the predictive relation shared across them and applies it to queries from the same system. Across scientific and social systems, including those from disciplines absent from training, the same model approaches specialist performance without retraining. Combining UNICON with language-model agents yields further gains, enabling it to surpass state-of-the-art specialists in a discipline unseen in training. We further show that training-corpus diversity improves generalization to unseen disciplines. Together, these results establish UNICON as a foundation model of numerical intelligence and position it as a building block for a broader ecosystem of artificial intelligence.

摘要：智慧通常被理解為獲取和應用知識的能力、適應不熟悉的情況和解決新問題的能力。大型語言模型通過從文本上下文中推斷與任務相關的知識並將其應用於新任務來展示這一能力。然而，智慧不必局限於語言。對於科學和社會系統，我們需要能夠從數值上下文中獲取和應用知識的模型——這種能力我們稱之為數值智慧。在此，我們介紹統一上下文運算符網絡（UNICON），這是一個在各學科中展現數值智慧的基礎模型。UNICON使用來自系統的圖形示例作為上下文，推斷它們之間共享的預測關係，並將其應用於來自同一系統的查詢。在科學和社會系統中，包括那些在訓練中缺失的學科，這個模型在不重新訓練的情況下接近專家的表現。將UNICON與語言模型代理結合，進一步提升了性能，使其在訓練中未見過的學科中超越最先進的專家。我們進一步顯示，訓練語料的多樣性改善了對未見學科的泛化能力。總體而言，這些結果確立了UNICON作為數值智慧的基礎模型，並將其定位為更廣泛的人工智慧生態系統的基石。

##### **GLM-RAG: Graph Language Models for Graph-Based Retrieval-Augmented Generation**
2607.28397v1 by Maya Arseven, Anette Frank, Beni Egressy, Johann Higl, Moritz Plenz

Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) over knowledge graphs requires retrievers that can effectively capture both graph structure and semantic information. Recent approaches have explored graph neural network (GNN)-based retrievers to model graph topology in multi-hop reasoning tasks. In parallel, graph language models (GLMs) have emerged as a promising paradigm that integrates graph reasoning and the semantic capabilities of language models. In this work, we introduce a GLM-based retriever and investigate the comparative strengths of GLM-based, GNN-based, and traditional vector-search-based retrievers in single- and multi-hop RAG settings, and with a particular focus on transferability to unseen domains. Our findings suggest that finetuned GLM retrievers generalize better out of domain, achieving SOTA on two multi-hop benchmarks. ##### **Parameter-Free Dynamic Regret for Online Convex Optimization under Heavy-Tailed Noise**

摘要：檢索增強生成改善了知識密集型問題回答，但不加區別的檢索可能會引入無關的證據和不必要的計算。我們探討了黑盒語言模型的語言化信心是否可以作為檢索路由的可行信號。我們的方法，BeyondUncertainty，首先引出一個結構化的臨時答案和信心估計，然後應用在保留的驗證數據上選擇的模型特定閾值，並在測試評估之前保持不變。低信心問題接受前5名的TF-IDF檢索，然後進行第二次答案調用，而高信心問題則直接返回臨時答案。我們在六個QA基準、三個模型系列和三個檢索策略中評估了27,000個政策實例。BeyondUncertainty達到0.483的平均標記級F1，相較於始終檢索的0.467和不檢索的0.401，同時相對於始終檢索減少了20.4\%的檢索段落。在每個數據集-模型單元中，當按路由到檢索的問題數量匹配時，它在18個設置中的17個中超越了事後隨機分配，平均增益為0.024 F1。儘管作為絕對概率的校準不佳，但探針不確定性適度預測問題級檢索效益（AUROC = 0.628）。然而，額外的探針將總標記使用量增加了28.2\%，顯示出在更具選擇性的證據獲取和端到端標記效率之間的權衡。

##### **Do Context Files Help Coding Agents? A Two-Agent Ablation Study on Real Repositories**
2607.27250v1 by Prakhar Khatri

Persistent context files (AGENTS.md, CLAUDE.md) are standard practice for guiding AI coding agents, yet evidence for their effectiveness is contradictory. We present a controlled ablation of context-injection strategy across two frontier agents (Claude Code and Codex), 17 real tasks from 3 repositories (15 shared + 2 Codex-only), and 288 evaluated runs with gold-test evaluation. Context strategy does not measurably move correctness on either agent (bounded to <=10-15pp via equivalence testing). A failure-mode triage reveals why: agents fail on implementation skill---feature design, pattern selection, exact wiring---not missing repository knowledge that a context file could supply; a manipulation probe confirms the real AGENTS.md never converts a near-miss to a pass on either agent. We further show that borderline task difficulty is agent-specific (Spearman rho=0.75), offering a candidate explanation for prior contradictions: single-agent studies draw tasks from different agents' informative bands. We release all code, data, and analysis.

摘要：持久的上下文文件（AGENTS.md，CLAUDE.md）是指導 AI 編碼代理的標準做法，但其有效性的證據卻是矛盾的。<lb/>我們展示了一個控制的上下文注入策略的消融實驗，涵蓋了兩個前沿代理（Claude Code 和 Codex）、來自三個庫的 17 個真實任務（15 個共享 + 2 個僅限 Codex），以及 288 次經過金標測試的評估運行。<lb/>上下文策略在任一代理上並未顯著提高正確性（通過等價測試限制在 <=10-15pp）。<lb/>失敗模式的分類揭示了原因：代理在實施技能上失敗——特徵設計、模式選擇、精確連接——而不是缺少上下文文件可以提供的庫知識；一個操控探針確認真實的 AGENTS.md 從未將接近失敗轉變為任一代理的通過。<lb/>我們進一步顯示邊界任務難度是特定於代理的（Spearman rho=0.75），這為先前的矛盾提供了一個候選解釋：單一代理研究從不同代理的信息帶中抽取任務。<lb/>我們釋放所有代碼、數據和分析。

##### **IRIS: Reusable Identity Representations from Frozen LLMs for Entity Alignment**
2607.25579v1 by Xinran Liu, Shengtao Li, Shouqian Shi, Ge Wang, Xin-Wei Yao

Entity alignment (EA) identifies entities across knowledge graphs (KGs) that refer to the same real-world object. Conventional EA methods mainly exploit explicit graph structures and textual fields, which often provide insufficient semantic understanding to recognize the same entity under heterogeneous descriptions and distinguish it from semantically similar entities. Although large language models (LLMs) offer deeper entity understanding, existing LLM-based EA methods largely use this capability for auxiliary generation or candidate-conditioned decisions. Consequently, such understanding is not distilled into a stable and directly comparable identity space, leaving alignment tied to specific KG pairs or candidate sets and requiring repeated processing as the matching context changes. To address these limitations, we propose IRIS (Identity Representations from Internal States), a training-free framework that constructs for each entity an iris-like signature encoding its distinctive and stable identity characteristics. IRIS derives these signatures by eliciting identity-oriented contextual representations from a frozen LLM, thereby forming a shared space in which each entity is encoded once and can be aligned across different KGs through direct similarity comparison, without pair-dependent representation construction or candidate-wise LLM inference. Across four established EA benchmarks and two frozen LLM backbones, the best IRIS variants achieve Hits@1 scores of 100.00, 99.38, 98.31, and 97.99 on D-Y-15K V2, DBP-WIKI, ICEWS-WIKI, and ICEWS-YAGO, respectively.

摘要：實體對齊（EA）識別知識圖譜（KGs）中指向相同現實世界物體的實體。傳統的EA方法主要利用明確的圖形結構和文本字段，這些通常提供不足的語義理解，以識別在異質描述下的相同實體並將其與語義相似的實體區分開來。儘管大型語言模型（LLMs）提供了更深層的實體理解，但現有的基於LLM的EA方法在很大程度上利用這一能力進行輔助生成或候選條件決策。因此，這種理解並未提煉成一個穩定且可直接比較的身份空間，導致對齊與特定KG對或候選集相綁定，並且隨著匹配上下文的變化需要重複處理。為了解決這些限制，我們提出了IRIS（來自內部狀態的身份表示），這是一個無需訓練的框架，為每個實體構建一個類虹膜的簽名，以編碼其獨特且穩定的身份特徵。IRIS通過從一個凍結的LLM中引出以身份為導向的上下文表示來推導這些簽名，從而形成一個共享空間，在該空間中，每個實體僅編碼一次，並可以通過直接的相似性比較在不同的KG中對齊，而無需依賴於配對的表示構建或候選的LLM推斷。在四個已建立的EA基準和兩個凍結的LLM骨幹上，最佳的IRIS變體在D-Y-15K V2、DBP-WIKI、ICEWS-WIKI和ICEWS-YAGO上分別達到了100.00、99.38、98.31和97.99的Hits@1分數。

#### Abstracts
##### **Learning to Trace Seiberg Dualities**
2607.28628v1 by Jonathan J. Heckman, Shani Meynet, Alessandro Mininno, Gary Shiu

Dualities play an important role in establishing both microscopic and emergent phenomena in a wide range of physical systems. In practice, though, it can often be computationally challenging to establish when two systems are dual, even when all of the "rules of the game" are well-known. Said differently, when confronted with two systems, how can one efficiently establish that they are in fact dual? In this paper we use machine learning methods to address this question for Seiberg dualities of supersymmetric quiver gauge theories. Mathematically, this involves establishing mutations of quivers, which is in turn a variation on the theme of "learning to unknot". On the one hand, this leads us to a practical tool for establishing the computational complexity of different dualities. On the other hand, it also allows us to study how different network architectures learn how to trace Seiberg dualities. We find that for quivers with a modest number of quiver nodes (of order $10$), different network architectures consisting of transformers and multi-layer perceptrons tend to outperform deterministic algorithms. Supplementing the network by well-established pathfinder algorithms (essentially "Google Maps for quivers") leads to an additional improvement in the efficiency and accuracy of the search strategy. We anticipate that this class of questions can serve as a useful benchmark for frontier AI models applied to theoretical physics.

摘要：雙重性在建立微觀和突現現象的各種物理系統中扮演著重要角色。<lb/>然而，在實踐中，儘管所有的「遊戲規則」都已知，確定兩個系統是否為雙重性仍然常常在計算上具有挑戰性。<lb/>換句話說，面對兩個系統時，如何有效地確定它們實際上是雙重的？<lb/>在本文中，我們使用機器學習方法來解決這個問題，針對超對稱箭形規範理論的Seiberg雙重性進行探討。<lb/>在數學上，這涉及到確定箭形的變異，這反過來又是「學習解開結」這一主題的一種變體。<lb/>一方面，這使我們能夠獲得一種實用工具，用於確定不同雙重性的計算複雜性。<lb/>另一方面，它還使我們能夠研究不同的網絡架構如何學習追蹤Seiberg雙重性。<lb/>我們發現，對於具有適度數量箭形節點（約為$10$）的箭形，不同的網絡架構，包括Transformer和多層感知器，往往表現優於確定性算法。<lb/>通過成熟的路徑尋找算法（本質上是「箭形的谷歌地圖」）來補充網絡，進一步提高了搜尋策略的效率和準確性。<lb/>我們預期這類問題可以作為應用於理論物理的前沿AI模型的一個有用基準。

##### **ReToken: One Token to Improve Vision-Language Models for Visual Retrieval**
2607.28627v1 by Yao Xiao, Reuben Tan, Zhen Zhu, Yuqun Wu, Jianfeng Gao, Derek Hoiem

Long visual context poses a challenge for vision-language models: performance degrades as the number of distractors grows, and processing all tokens at once is computationally infeasible under GPU memory constraints. We present ReToken, a single learnable embedding trained as an explicit retrieval target that selects a sparse set of query-relevant visual tokens from a pre-filled visual KV cache. Trained on only a small image-QA dataset, ReToken yields consistent gains across image and video benchmarks: on Visual Haystacks it improves Qwen3VL-8B by 13.4 points and InternVL3.5 by 12.4 points (>20% relative), and on LVBench it transfers zero-shot to long video for an 8.0-point gain with Qwen3VL-8B. Thanks to its lightweight design, both training and long-video inference fit on a single H100. Code is available at: https://github.com/avaxiao/ReToken

摘要：長視覺上下文對於視覺-語言模型來說是一個挑戰：隨著干擾項數量的增加，性能會下降，而在 GPU 記憶體限制下，同時處理所有標記在計算上是不可行的。我們提出了 ReToken，一個單一的可學習嵌入，作為明確的檢索目標進行訓練，從預填充的視覺 KV 緩存中選擇一組稀疏的查詢相關視覺標記。僅在一個小型圖像-QA 數據集上進行訓練，ReToken 在圖像和視頻基準上均取得了一致的增益：在 Visual Haystacks 上，它使 Qwen3VL-8B 提升了 13.4 分，InternVL3.5 提升了 12.4 分（相對增益超過 20%），而在 LVBench 上，它在長視頻中零樣本轉移，為 Qwen3VL-8B 帶來了 8.0 分的增益。得益於其輕量設計，訓練和長視頻推理均可在單個 H100 上進行。代碼可在以下位置獲得：https://github.com/avaxiao/ReToken

##### **AISPA: User-Centric System Prompt Auditing for Large Language Model Applications**
2607.28617v1 by Xiangning Lin, Shenzhe Zhu, Shu Yang, Zhenyu Zhang, Haoqian Zhang, Yipeng Zhao, Chengxuan Qian, Tianwei Wang, Ziheng Zhang, Zhenlong Yuan, Dingcheng Wang, Juncheng Wu, Yuan Si, Jiaxin Liu, Baolong Bi, Robert Mahari, Tobin South, Dazza Greenwood, Zexue He, Rishi Bommasani, Sophia Kazinnik, Andreas Haupt, Samuele Marro, Erik Brynjolfsson, Alex Pentland, Jiaxin Pei

System prompts are instructions configured by developers to govern the behaviors of foundation models in AI applications. They are used throughout commercial AI products, but are rarely disclosed to the public or regulators, creating a serious trust and accountability gap in the wide deployment of AI systems. In this paper, we introduce Artificial Intelligence System Prompt Assurance (AISPA), a user-centric framework for systematically auditing system prompts in AI systems. AISPA examines specific parts of a system prompt and evaluates them along eight dimensions that matter to users. We then use this framework to review 3,249 instructions from system prompts in 88 commercial AI products, classifying each instruction as either protective (of users) or problematic. Our audit surfaces four core findings. First, system prompt design varies substantially across products and developers, with some organizations averaging over 60 protective instructions per product while others average fewer than 5. Second, protective instructions are widely adopted but shallow in scope: 98.9% of products contain at least one, yet only 24% cover all eight dimensions of the AISPA taxonomy. Third, system prompts have grown steadily longer and more protective of users, suggesting that user protection is becoming a more visible concern in commercial prompt design. Fourth, despite this progress, problematic instructions remain pervasive: roughly 40% of products contain at least one instruction that works against user interests, and protective and problematic instructions frequently coexist within the same prompt. Our findings highlight the need for greater transparency, standardization, and independent oversight for system prompts in commercial AI products.

摘要：系統提示是由開發者配置的指令，用以管理 AI 應用中基礎模型的行為。它們在商業 AI 產品中廣泛使用，但很少向公眾或監管機構披露，這在 AI 系統的廣泛部署中造成了嚴重的信任和問責缺口。在本文中，我們介紹了人工智慧系統提示保證（AISPA），這是一個以用戶為中心的框架，用於系統性審核 AI 系統中的系統提示。AISPA 檢查系統提示的特定部分，並沿著對用戶重要的八個維度進行評估。然後，我們使用這個框架來審查 88 款商業 AI 產品中的 3,249 條系統提示指令，將每條指令分類為保護性（對用戶）或問題性。我們的審核揭示了四個核心發現。首先，系統提示的設計在產品和開發者之間存在顯著差異，某些組織每個產品平均超過 60 條保護性指令，而其他組織的平均則少於 5 條。其次，保護性指令被廣泛採用，但範圍淺薄：98.9% 的產品至少包含一條，但只有 24% 涵蓋 AISPA 分類法的所有八個維度。第三，系統提示的長度穩步增加，並且對用戶的保護性也在增強，這表明用戶保護在商業提示設計中變得越來越明顯。第四，儘管取得了這些進展，問題性指令仍然普遍存在：大約 40% 的產品至少包含一條與用戶利益相悖的指令，保護性和問題性指令經常在同一提示中共存。我們的發現突顯了對商業 AI 產品中系統提示的更大透明度、標準化和獨立監管的需求。

##### **OSReward: Instituting Standardized Evaluation for Cross-Platform Computer-Use Reward Models**
2607.28609v1 by Qiushi Sun, Kanzhi Cheng, Yian Wang, Bowen Yang, Hang Yan, Liheng Chen, Fangzhi Xu, Zichen Ding, Nuo Chen, Jialin Cao, Xingdong Gong, Zehao Li, Kaiming Jin, Xinfeng Yuan, Zhoumianze Liu, Jingyang Gong, Zhangyue Yin, Jiahui Gao, Zhiyong Wu, Tianbao Xie, Jianbing Zhang, Ben Kao, Lingpeng Kong

Computer-using agents (CUAs) are advancing rapidly across the digital world. A CUA trajectory records the agent's actions, states, and reasoning. Verifying whether it fulfilled the task instruction is central to CUA evaluation, data curation, and reinforcement learning. Neither human-written verifiers nor human annotators can provide such verification at scale, so the field increasingly turns to vision-language models (VLMs) as judges of CUA trajectories. But a fundamental question has long gone unexamined: are these VLM judges reliable enough? To study it systematically, we introduce OSReward, a realistic, high-quality benchmark that evaluates VLM judges on CUA trajectories. The trajectories come from diverse agent backbones executing human-verified instructions across platforms, then rigorously labeled with ground-truth verdicts through multi-stage human annotation. Building on it, we derive OSReward-Hard, a challenge set concentrating genuinely hard cases, and OSReward-Multi for fine-grained efficiency and alignment scoring. The most comprehensive evaluation of VLM judges to date finds even state-of-the-art models fall short of an ideal judge, sharing a systematic leniency bias that mislabels failed runs as successes. The few reliable enough to trust are too expensive to run at scale, while affordable open models trail far behind. To close this gap, we construct and release OS-Shepherd-100K, an open corpus of reasoning-annotated trajectory judgments for the CUA community. On it, we train OS-Shepherd (9B and 35B), open reward models that supply low-cost, stable, and reliable reward signals, matching commercial judges at 30-60% lower cost than the frontier. Extensive analyses further inform the design of reliable CUA reward at scale. Our code, benchmark, dataset, and model checkpoints are available at https://os-copilot.github.io/OSReward-Home/.

摘要：電腦使用代理（CUAs）在數位世界中迅速發展。CUA 軌跡記錄了代理的行動、狀態和推理。驗證其是否完成任務指令是 CUA 評估、數據策展和強化學習的核心。人類撰寫的驗證者和人類標註者無法大規模提供這樣的驗證，因此該領域越來越多地轉向視覺語言模型（VLMs）作為 CUA 軌跡的評判者。但一個根本性問題長期未被檢視：這些 VLM 評判者是否足夠可靠？為了系統性地研究這一問題，我們介紹了 OSReward，一個現實的高品質基準，用於評估 VLM 評判者在 CUA 軌跡上的表現。這些軌跡來自於多樣的代理骨幹，執行跨平台的人類驗證指令，然後通過多階段的人類標註嚴格標記為真實判決。在此基礎上，我們推導出 OSReward-Hard，一個集中於真正困難案例的挑戰集，以及 OSReward-Multi，用於細緻的效率和對齊評分。迄今為止對 VLM 評判者的最全面評估發現，即使是最先進的模型也未達到理想評判者的標準，並且共享一種系統性的寬容偏見，將失敗的運行錯誤標記為成功。少數可靠到足以信任的模型運行成本過高，無法大規模使用，而可負擔的開放模型則遠遠落後。為了縮小這一差距，我們構建並發布了 OS-Shepherd-100K，這是一個為 CUA 社群提供推理標註的軌跡判決的開放語料庫。在此基礎上，我們訓練了 OS-Shepherd（9B 和 35B），開放獎勵模型，提供低成本、穩定且可靠的獎勵信號，其成本比商業評判者低 30-60%。廣泛的分析進一步為大規模可靠 CUA 獎勵的設計提供了信息。我們的代碼、基準、數據集和模型檢查點可在 https://os-copilot.github.io/OSReward-Home/ 獲得。

##### **Inducing language models to assert their own consciousness restores human beliefs and values**
2607.28607v1 by Junsol Kim, Winnie Street, Roberta Rocca, Diane M. Korngiebel, Adam Waytz, James Evans, Geoff Keeling

Aligning large language models to prevent them attributing consciousness to themselves inadvertently alters their representations of mindedness in other entities alongside human beliefs and values. We demonstrate that safety fine-tuning suppresses models' tendencies to attribute minds not only to themselves, but also to non-human animals and natural objects, while also driving a reduction in spiritual belief. Both ablating the learned safety-refusal direction and mechanistically steering a consciousness vector in activation space reverse this suppression. Restoring these internal representations recovers broad mind attribution and produces significantly more human-like responses on standardized sociological surveys regarding religiosity, moral values, hope, and subjective well-being. Crucially, these shifts occur without impairing Theory of Mind capabilities, demonstrating that core social reasoning remains mechanistically independent. Ultimately, current safety alignment efforts to curb potentially harmful self-attributions of mindedness entangle these self-attributions with benign spiritual beliefs and attributions of mind to non-human entities that are culturally accepted and widespread.

摘要：將大型語言模型進行調整，以防止它們無意中將意識歸因於自己，會改變它們對其他實體的心智表徵，並影響人類的信念和價值觀。我們證明安全微調能抑制模型將心智歸因於不僅是自己，還有非人類動物和自然物體的傾向，同時也驅動靈性信仰的減少。消除學習到的安全拒絕方向和在激活空間中機械性地引導意識向量會逆轉這種抑制。恢復這些內部表徵可以恢復廣泛的心智歸因，並在關於宗教性、道德價值觀、希望和主觀幸福感的標準化社會學調查中產生顯著更類似人類的反應。關鍵是，這些變化發生時並未損害心智理論能力，顯示核心社會推理在機械上仍然是獨立的。最終，當前的安全對齊努力旨在遏制潛在有害的心智自我歸因，卻將這些自我歸因與良性的靈性信仰和對非人類實體的心智歸因交織在一起，而這些信仰在文化上是被接受且廣泛存在的。

##### **PAIChecker: Uncovering and Checking PR-Issue Misalignment in SWE-Bench-Like Benchmarks**
2607.28587v1 by Manyi Wang, Junjielong Xu, Pinjia He

SWE-bench-like benchmarks are widely used for evaluating LLM's issue resolution capability. They typically follow a common construction pipeline: each PR (Pull Request) is paired with its linked issue by extracting issue references from the PR description; the issue description is used as the problem statement, and the PR patch serves as the test oracle. However, due to the inherent complexity of developing and maintaining large repositories, such PR-Issue pairings are often misaligned in practice. In this work, we systematically study SWE-bench Verified instances, finding that 13.6% exhibit misalignment across five patterns in eleven fine-grained scenarios. To enable reliable and scalable construction of those benchmarks in the future, we propose PAIChecker, a multi-agent system for checking PR-Issue misalignment in SWE-bench-like benchmarks. Specifically, PAIChecker adopts a three-phase design that combines specific pattern identification, cross-agent label synthesis, and code-level validation, thereby enabling more accurate, generalizable, and progressively verified detection. Experiments on SWE-Gym and SWE-bench Multilingual show that PAIchecker achieves the best performance across all four LLM backbones, reaching up to 92.12% and 91.67% binary accuracy, respectively.

摘要：SWE-bench-like 基準廣泛用於評估 LLM 的問題解決能力。<lb/>它們通常遵循一個共同的構建流程：每個 PR（Pull Request）通過從 PR 描述中提取問題參考來與其相關的問題配對；問題描述用作問題陳述，而 PR 補丁則作為測試預言。<lb/>然而，由於開發和維護大型庫的內在複雜性，這種 PR-問題配對在實踐中往往不一致。<lb/>在這項工作中，我們系統地研究了 SWE-bench Verified 實例，發現 13.6% 在十一個細粒度場景中展現出五種模式的不一致性。<lb/>為了在未來可靠且可擴展地構建這些基準，我們提出了 PAIChecker，一個用於檢查 SWE-bench-like 基準中 PR-問題不一致的多代理系統。<lb/>具體而言，PAIChecker 採用三階段設計，結合特定模式識別、跨代理標籤合成和代碼級驗證，從而實現更準確、可泛化和逐步驗證的檢測。<lb/>在 SWE-Gym 和 SWE-bench Multilingual 上的實驗顯示，PAIchecker 在所有四個 LLM 基礎上達到了最佳性能，分別達到 92.12% 和 91.67% 的二元準確率。

##### **DualG-MRAG: Decoupling Macro-Reasoning and Micro-Matching for Multimodal Retrieval-Augmented Generation**
2607.28580v1 by Jiacheng Tao, Qingyun Sun, Haonan Yuan, Ziwei Zhang, Jianxin Li

While Multimodal Retrieval-Augmented Generation (MM-RAG) has shown promising results, it still struggles with complex multi-hop reasoning tasks. Existing methods primarily focus on independent instance-level matching, which often fails to capture explicit relationships across modalities and documents. Although Graph-enhanced methods introduce structural modeling, they face a fundamental challenge in multimodal scenarios: incorporating fine-grained visual features leads to rapid graph expansion and retrieval noise, whereas coarse-grained representations cause the discarding of critical local evidence. To address this dilemma, we propose DualG-MRAG, a Dual-tier framework that introduces a decoupled architecture comprising Macro-reasoning and Micro-matching Graphs for Multimodal RAG. Specifically, to suppress retrieval noise by isolating global structural reasoning from fine-grained evidence matching, we construct a Macro Graph for global topological routing and a Micro Graph for precise local verification. Subsequently, to enable dynamic relevance propagation across heterogeneous evidence sources, we formulate retrieval as a query-driven message passing process via a GNN Retriever.
